\begin{center}\Large AL-BATTĀNĪ\par\vspace{4mm}\LARGE OPUS ASTRONOMICUM\par
\vspace{5mm}\large Caroli Alphonsi Nallino versio Latina et adnotationes (pars I)\par\vspace{8mm}
\Large S04 --- editio diplomatica\par\vspace{4mm}\large Paginae impressae 219--235\end{center}
\vspace{12mm}\noindent Singulae lineae paginarum impressarum singulis lineis huius editionis respondent, cum
divisionibus vocabulorum, signis et generibus litterarum (rectis, inclinatis, distantibus). Numeri linearum et
paginae textus Arabici in marginibus stant ut impressi sunt. Textus ex imaginibus paginarum transcriptus est;
litterae Graecae et Arabicae, numeri et signa in imaginibus amplificatis lecta sunt.
Haec transcriptio a nullo homine recognita est.\par

\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L004}{}{}{\Name{Aḥmad}, unus eorum trium, anno 220 Iezdegerdis (inc. 23 Apr. 851 Chr.), teste}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L005}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.61mm}\smash{\rlap{\Name{Ibn Yūnus}, l. c., et cap. VIII (ap. \Name{Delambre}, p. 99)}}}\rlap{\hspace*{134.36mm}\smash{\llap{84}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{140.29mm}\smash{\llap{33}\rlap{′}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L006}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}\smash{\rlap{Familia \Name{Banū Amāǵūr} (\Name{Ibn Yūnus} cap. V, \Name{Caussin} p. 152)}}}\rlap{\hspace*{134.36mm}\smash{\llap{83}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{140.29mm}\smash{\llap{35}\rlap{′}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L007}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}\smash{\rlap{\Name{Mufliḥ ibn Yūsuf}, libertus illius familiae (\Name{Ibn Yūnus} l. c.)}}}\rlap{\hspace*{134.36mm}\smash{\llap{84}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{140.29mm}\smash{\llap{5}\rlap{′}}}}
\par\vspace{1.14mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0308-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L008}{}{}{\Indent Addantur duae illae \Name{Persicae} observationes (?) quas, ex opere Ibn Yūnus sumptas,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L009}{}{}{retuli p. 209, adn. 2; posterior, anni 630 Chr. circiter, 80° praebuit ut tabulae \textit{ash–Shāh} (e}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L010}{}{}{Pehlevico seu Persico aevi medii in Arabicum translatae) et prior \textit{Sūrya–siddhānta.}}
\par\vspace{3.40mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0308-H11}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L011}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{d}}) — \textit{Pag. 46, l. 7.}}}
\par\vspace{3.36mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0308-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L012}{}{}{\Indent Longitudinem lineae EF servavi ut est in cod. Escurialensi et in versione Platonis, nempe}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L013}{}{}{61\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 48′ 35″. Exactius erat:}
\par\vspace{0.35mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0308-H14}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L014}{}{}{\CenterLine{$EF = \sqrt{EM^2 + FM^2} = \sqrt{(61^{p}\,48'\,2'')^2 + (1^{p}\,2'\,22''\,\qfrac{1}{2})^2} =$}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0308-H15}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L015}{}{}{\CenterLine{$= \sqrt{(222482'')^2 + (3472'',5)^2} = \sqrt{49\;498\;240\;324^{\text{IV}},00 + 14\;006\;306^{\text{IV}},25} =$}}
\par\vspace{-0.14mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0308-H16}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L016}{}{}{\CenterLine{$= \sqrt{49\;512\;246\;630^{\text{IV}},25} = 222\;513'',47... = 61^{p}\,48'\,33'',47.$}}
\par\vspace{3.61mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0308-H17}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L017}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{e}}) — \textit{Pag. 46, l. 25–26.}}}
\par\vspace{2.19mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0308-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L018}{}{}{\Indent Longitudo lineae EG ut in cod. et apud Platonem, nempe 58\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 12′ 34″; sed p. 47, l. 32,}
\par\vspace{0.14mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L019}{}{}{cod. rectius secundas 32″ habet. Nam}
\par\vspace{0.21mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0308-H20}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L020}{}{}{\CenterLine{$EG = \sqrt{KG^2 + EK^2} = \sqrt{(1^{p}\,2'\,22''\,\qfrac{1}{2})^2 + (58^{p}\,11'\,58'')^2} =$}}
\par\vspace{-0.12mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0308-H21}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L021}{}{}{\CenterLine{$= \sqrt{(3742'',5)^2 + (209\;518'')^2} = \sqrt{14\;006\;306^{\text{IV}},25 + 43\;897\;792\;324^{\text{IV}}} =$}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0308-H22}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L022}{}{}{\CenterLine{$= \sqrt{43\;911\;798\;625^{\text{IV}},25} = 209\;551'',4... = 58^{p}\,12'\,31'',4.$}}
\par\vspace{3.51mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0308-H23}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L023}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{f}}) — \textit{Pag. 48, l. 2-16.}}}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.391\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0308-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0308-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.589\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.411\FullSourceWidth}
\par\vspace{3.30mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0308-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Aequationem centri} supputare.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0308-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L025}{}{}{\Indent Constructiones geometricae p. 46–47 ex}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L026}{}{}{\textit{Almag.} III, 5 (ed. Halma t. I, p. 190–199)}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L027}{}{}{sumptae sunt; deest contra apud Ptolemaeum}
\par\vspace{0.14mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L028}{}{}{formula \Name{al–Battānī}:}
\par\vspace{1.85mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L029}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{1.64mm}sin aeq. centri $= \dfrac{R\,\dfrac{e \sin m}{R}}{\sqrt{\Bigl(\dfrac{e \sin m}{R}\Bigr)^{2} + \Bigl(\dfrac{e \cos m}{R} + 60^{p}\Bigr)^{2}}}$\,,}}
\par\vspace{0.22mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L030}{}{}{ubi \textit{e} est excentricitas vel, in hypothesi epi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L031}{}{}{cycli, radius epicycli (1); \textit{m} arcus anomaliae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L032}{}{}{scilicet arcus excentrici vel epicycli quem sidus}
\par\vspace{0.11mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L033}{}{}{ab apogeo usque ad datum temporis momen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-body-L034}{}{}{tum percurrerit. Facillime regula deducitur e}
\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{0.76mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{0.04mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-notes_left-L001}{}{}{supputassent, longitudinem apogei 79° 30′ ponebant.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-notes_left-L002}{}{}{Est quantitas recepta in eorum \textit{Tabula Probata} et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-notes_left-L003}{}{}{ab \Name{Ḥabash}, f. 28,r. — Perperam \Name{al-Mas‘ūdī},}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\textit{at-Tanbīh} p. 222, hanc observationem anno 217 heg.}
\par\vspace{-0.13mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-notes_right-L002}{}{}{fuisse scribit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0308-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0308-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (1) In partibus radii excentrici vel orbitae ($=$60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}).}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0309}{220}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 0.19mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L001}{}{}{constructionibus Ptolemaicis et Albatenianis; in hypothesi epicycli, ubi aequatio centri $=$}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L002}{}{}{ang. HEF,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L003}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{15.67mm}$\sin HEF : MF = \sin FME : EF$,\hspace{3.5mm}id est\hspace{3.5mm}sin aeq. centri $: MF = \sin 90° : EF$,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L004}{}{}{et quoniam sin 90° $=$ \textit{R}, erit}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0309-H05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L005}{}{}{\CenterLine{sin aeq. centri $= R\,\dfrac{MF}{EF}$.}}
\par\vspace{0.35mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L006}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.80mm}1)}\rlap{\hspace*{30.15mm}$EF = \sqrt{\overline{MF}^2 + (AE + AM)^2} = \sqrt{\overline{MF}^2 + (60^{p} + AM)^2}$.}}
\par\vspace{-0.52mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0309-H07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L007}{}{}{\CenterLine{$AM : AF = \sin AFM : \sin AMF = \cos HAF : R$}}
\par\vspace{0.11mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L008}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.66mm}unde}\rlap{\hspace*{47.42mm}$AM = \dfrac{AF \times \cos HAF}{R} = \dfrac{e \cos m}{R}$.}}
\par\vspace{0.64mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0309-H09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L009}{}{}{\CenterLine{$MF : AF = \sin HAF : \sin AMF$}}
\par\vspace{0.13mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L010}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.56mm}unde}\rlap{\hspace*{47.42mm}$MF = \dfrac{AF \times \sin HAF}{R} = \dfrac{e \sin m}{R}$.}}
\par\vspace{0.54mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0309-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L011}{}{}{\Indent Valores isti linearum AM, MF introducantur in 1), proveniet}
\par\vspace{-0.13mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0309-H12}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L012}{}{}{\CenterLine{$EF = \sqrt{\Bigl(\dfrac{e \sin m}{R}\Bigr)^2 + \Bigl(\dfrac{e \cos m}{R} + 60^{p}\Bigr)^2}$}}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L013}{}{}{quod est denominator formulae Albatenianae.}
\par\vspace{0.14mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0309-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L014}{}{}{\Indent Similiter in hypothesi excentrici:}
\par\vspace{0.32mm}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.407\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0309-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0309-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.573\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.427\FullSourceWidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L015}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{3.81mm}sin aequat. centri $= \sin LME = R\,\dfrac{LE}{ME}$}}
\par\vspace{1.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L016}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.59mm}$LE = HE\dfrac{\sin LHE}{R} = HE\dfrac{\sin FHM}{R} = \dfrac{e \sin m}{R}$}}
\par\vspace{1.14mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L017}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.52mm}$LH = HE\dfrac{\cos LHE}{R} = HE\dfrac{\cos FHM}{R} = \dfrac{e \cos m}{R}$}}
\par\vspace{0.18mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L018}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{6.82mm}$ME = \sqrt{\overline{LE}^2 + (HL + 60^{p})^2}$ etc.}}
\par\vspace{-0.65mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0309-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L019}{}{}{\Indent Si tangentium cognovisset usum, sim-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L020}{}{}{plicius invenisset in hypothesi epicycli}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L021}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.00mm}tang aequat. $=$ tang $HEF = R\,\dfrac{MF}{60^{p} + AM} =$}}
\par\vspace{-0.17mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L022}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{16.29mm}$= R\,\dfrac{e \sin m}{60^{p} R + e \cos m}$,}}
\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{0.23mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L023}{}{}{et in hypothesi excentrici}
\par\vspace{0.24mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0309-H24}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L024}{}{}{\CenterLine{tang aequat. $=$ tang $LME = R\,\dfrac{LE}{60^{p} + HL} = R\,\dfrac{e \sin m}{60^{p} R + e \cos m}$.}}
\par\vspace{2.48mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0309-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L025}{}{}{\Indent In omnibus his formulis excentricitas vel radius epicycli, scilicet \textit{e}, est in partibus \textit{sexa-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L026}{}{}{\textit{gesimis} radii excentrici vel orbitae, qui 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} complecti ponitur. At radio excentrici vel orbitae}
\par\vspace{1.11mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L027}{}{}{1\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} sumpto, et \textit{e} in eius partibus \textit{decimalibus,} ut noster est mos, expressa (seu posita $\frac{e}{60}$}
\par\vspace{1.22mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L028}{}{}{pro \textit{e} Graecorum et Albatenii) manifeste pro 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} ubique erit 1 scribendum.}
\par\vspace{6.75mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0309-H29}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L029}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{g}}) — \textit{Pag. 48, l. 17-20.}}}
\par\vspace{4.66mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0309-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L030}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Radii epicyclorum} Lunae et quinque planetarum.}
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0309-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L031}{}{}{\Indent Correctio numerorum facilis est, nam hi radii sunt sinus maximae aequationis centri (in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L032}{}{}{media distantia a Terra, si de quinque planetis agitur). Aequationes maximae (mediae in pla-}
\par\vspace{0.12mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0309-body-L033}{}{}{netis) et earum sinus e tabulis al–Battānī sumantur; proveniet:}
\DSource{0310}{221}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0310-body-L017}{}{}{\Indent Ut \Name{Ptolemaeus}, ita omnes mihi noti \Name{Arabici} astronomi diem a meridie supputant.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0310-body-L018}{}{}{\Name{Sédillot} tamen (ex I aut III inedito cap. \Name{Ibn Yūnus}?), p. 228 versionis \Name{Oloug Beg}, ait}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0310-body-L019}{}{}{\Name{Yaḥyà ibn Abī Manṣūr}, praecipuum \textit{Tabulae Probatae} auctorem, a media nocte diem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0310-body-L020}{}{}{ut \Name{Hipparchum} incipere; idem, teste \Name{al–Bīrūnī}, \textit{Chron.} p. 6 text. et vers., fecit auctor Ta-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0310-body-L021}{}{}{bulae \textit{shahriyārān ash–shāh,} de qua iam p. 218, adn. 4 dixi.}
\par\vspace{5.23mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0310-H22}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0310-body-L022}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{b}}) — \textit{Pag. 49, l. 8-13.}}}
\par\vspace{4.39mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0310-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0310-body-L023}{}{}{\Indent [\textit{Temporis aequatio.} — In theoria \Name{Albateniana} huius aequationis quaedam sunt no-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0310-body-L024}{}{}{tatu digna. Iis temporibus (et hodie quoque fere) maxima Solis retardatio respectu sui motus}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0310-body-L025}{}{}{medii eveniebat circa 20° Aquarii; antecessio maxima paulo post initium Scorpii. Quod al–}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0310-body-L026}{}{}{–Battānī significare vult verbis « \textit{locus deminutionis fere a $\qfrac{2}{3}$ Aquarii} » etc. Astronomi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0310-body-L027}{}{}{hodierni tempus medium ita definiverunt ut medium locum obtineret inter oscillationes motus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0310-body-L028}{}{}{solaris, atque ut correctio adhibenda tempori vero ob medium inveniendum (scilicet aequatio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0310-body-L029}{}{}{temporis) interdum additiva interdum subtractiva esset.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0310-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0310-body-L030}{}{}{\Indent Aliter \Name{Ptolemaeus}, qui, ut postea in \textit{Tabulis manualibus} \Name{Theon} (cfr. \Name{Delambre},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0310-body-L031}{}{}{\textbf{\textit{Histoire de l’astronomie ancienne,}} Paris 1817, t. II, p. 630–632), cursum temporis medii}
\par\vspace{1.66mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{1.58mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0310-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0310-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Apud \Name{al–Farghānī} radius epicycli lu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0310-notes_left-L002}{}{}{naris est 6\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} $\qfrac{1}{3}$ tam in edit. Golii (cap. XVI, p. 65)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0310-notes_left-L003}{}{}{quam in versione Latina Norimbergae impressa}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{1.45mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0310-notes_right-L001}{}{}{(1537, fol. 16,v.). Hanc editionem secutus sum in}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0310-notes_right-L002}{}{}{radio epicycli Martis statuendo; edit. Golii 39\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} $\qfrac{1}{6}$}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0310-notes_right-L003}{}{}{praebet.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0311}{222}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 0.08mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L001}{}{}{ita determinavit ut tum cum tempore vero congrueret, cum tempus verum magis quam in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L002}{}{}{omnibus reliquis anni partibus progressus esset, id est in initio Scorpii. Tunc ambo tempora}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L003}{}{}{congruent; in reliquis omnibus anni partibus tempus verum retardabit respectu medii, et cor-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L004}{}{}{rectio huic adhibenda ad verum tempus reperiendum, seu \textit{aequatio temporis,} semper erit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L005}{}{}{additiva. Eius maxima quantitas igitur in 20° Aquarii contingebat, ut e tabula huius aequa-}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L006}{}{}{tionis colligitur, quam \Name{Delambre}, \textit{Astr. anc.,} II, 632, iuxta \Name{Ptolemaeum} et \Name{Theonem}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L007}{}{}{praebet.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0311-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L008}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Al–Battānī} contra, qua causa motus haud bene scio, tempus medium et verum tum sup-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L009}{}{}{ponit congruere, cum tempus verum in maxima sua retardatione exsistat, scilicet in 20° Aqua-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L010}{}{}{rii; hoc enim significant verba « \textit{iuxta hoc nychthemerum} [20° Aquarii] \textit{reliqua nychthe-}}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L011}{}{}{« \textit{mera anni in hoc libro supputa} ». Manifeste igitur in omnibus reliquis anni partibus tempus}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L012}{}{}{verum \textit{antecedet} medium; correctio vel aequatio semper erit subtractiva, et maximam attinget}
\par\vspace{-0.11mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L013}{}{}{quantitatem prope Scorpii initium, ut e tabula t. II, p. 64 patet.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0311-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L014}{}{}{\Indent Ambae viae ad eadem ducunt; sed tempus medium Ptolemaei tempus medium al–Battānī}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L015}{}{}{\textit{antecedit} tota aequatione maxima, quam Ptolemaeus 33\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} 30\textsuperscript{\textit{s}}, al–Battānī 31\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} 12\textsuperscript{\textit{s}} facit. Nam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L016}{}{}{tempus medium Ptolemaei cum vero congruit quando hoc est in maxima sua antecessione; Al-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L017}{}{}{batenii contra quando verum est in maxima retardatione. Qua re al–Battānī, ut tabulas suas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L018}{}{}{astronomicas cum Ptolemaei comparabiles redderet si in eas eodem temporis argumento in-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L019}{}{}{tretur, omnes suas epochas motuum caelestium tanto augere debuit quanto istis motibus con-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L020}{}{}{tingit in intervallo 31\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} 12\textsuperscript{\textit{s}}. Talis correctio negligenda est in omnibus planetarum motibus; in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L021}{}{}{Sole paulo maior 1′; in Luna autem 18′ attingit. Hoc saltem pacto quodammodo intelligi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L022}{}{}{potest, cur infra (l. 28–29) dicat \textit{se mediae longitudini Lunae in suis tabulis 18′ addidisse.}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L023}{}{}{Ubi tempus medium Albatenii 0\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 0\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} 0\textsuperscript{\textit{s}} est, Ptolemaei 0\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 32\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} 12\textsuperscript{\textit{s}} attingit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0311-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent Consequitur etiam tabulas \Name{Ptolemaei} et \Name{al–Battānī}, aequationem temporis quod attinet,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L025}{}{}{non posse inter se directe comparari. In altera enim aequatio nulla est cum maxima in altera.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L026}{}{}{Si al–Battānī tabulam suam condidisset elementis quibus Ptolemaeus usus erat, summa aequa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L027}{}{}{tionum amborum semper 33\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} 30\textsuperscript{\textit{s}} esset; sed haec summa semper paululum diversa est quo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L028}{}{}{niam aequatio Albateniana elementis nonnihilo diversis (alia obliquitate eclipticae, alia orbitae}
\par\vspace{0.11mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L029}{}{}{solaris excentricitate, alia anni tropici longitudine) nititur. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0311-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L030}{}{}{\Indent Iuvat nunc referre quae, ex III cap. \Name{Ibn Yūnus}, narrat \Name{Delambre} p. 98: « Il [Ibn}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L031}{}{}{« Yūnus] compte cinq méthodes différentes pour calculer l’équation du tems. La première est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L032}{}{}{« celle de Ptolémée. Théon nous l’a expliquée de façon à ne rien laisser à désirer. La se-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L033}{}{}{« conde est celle des auteurs des Tables qui commencent par le point de l’écliptique où l’équa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L034}{}{}{« tion est nulle. Suivant la troisième, on commence par le point où l’équation est soustractive}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L035}{}{}{« (et sans doute la plus grande); dans la quatrième, on commence la Table au point où l’équa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L036}{}{}{« tion est additive; enfin, dans la cinquième, on commence au point équinoxial du printemps.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L037}{}{}{« Toutes ces méthodes au fond n’en sont qu’une ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0311-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L038}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Ḥabash}, f. 16,v., usum Ptolemaei sequitur.}
\par\vspace{5.27mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0311-H39}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L039}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{c}}) — \textit{Pag. 49, l. 15 sqq.}}}
\par\vspace{4.30mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0311-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L040}{}{}{\Indent [« \textit{Medium locum Solis datum} » hic locum medium initialem significat; « \textit{locum me-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L041}{}{}{\textit{dium alterum} » locum medium ad quem Sol tunc temporis pervenerit. Manifeste, ut in \textit{Al-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L042}{}{}{\textit{magesto,} problema est: datum intervallum temporis veri corrigere ut cognoscatur intervallum}
\par\vspace{0.11mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L043}{}{}{temporis medii ei respondens. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{-0.13mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0311-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L044}{}{}{\Indent Praecepta et a \Name{Ptolemaeo}, \textit{Almag.} III, 8 (ed. Halma t. I, p. 207–210) et ab \Name{al–Bat-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0311-body-L045}{}{}{\Name{tānī} haud multum perspicue exponuntur. Longe melius in commentario \Name{Theonis}, quem De-}
\DSource{0312}{223}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 0.25mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L001}{}{}{\Name{lambre} in adnotationibus ad illum Almagesti locum (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 28 adnotationum) re-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L002}{}{}{fert. Hoc modo summatim describi possunt: datum veri temporis intervallum inter \textit{t} et \textit{t′} sit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L003}{}{}{in intervallum temporis medii convertendum. Pro quoque \textit{t} et \textit{t′} tempore e tabulis Solis dedu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L004}{}{}{cantur longitudines mediae et verae; differentia inter duas medias erit arcus eclipticae medio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L005}{}{}{motu peractus, differentia inter duas veras arcus vero motu descriptus. Ascensionum rectarum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L006}{}{}{intervalla quae duobus iis eclipticae arcubus respondent sumantur; exibunt arcus aequatoris}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L007}{}{}{qui tempus medium et tempus verum inter \textit{t} et \textit{t′} metiuntur. Differentia horum duorum aequa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L008}{}{}{toris arcuum, in partes horae aequinoctialis conversa, erit correctio temporis veri qua medium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L009}{}{}{invenitur, id est aequatio temporis; addetur tempori inaequali seu vero cum huius arcus aequa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L010}{}{}{torialis brevior fuerit medio, aut de tempore inaequali demetur, cum arcus huius longior me-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L011}{}{}{dio exstiterit. Exibit intervallum datum expressum nychthemeris mediis seu aequinoctialibus.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L012}{}{}{« Suivant les modernes », inquit \Name{Delambre} l. c., « l’\textit{équation} du temps est l’excès de la longi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L013}{}{}{« tude moyenne sur \textit{l’ascension droite vraie,} et cet excès converti en temps à raison de 15\textsuperscript{\textit{d}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L014}{}{}{« par heure, est l’\textit{équation} du temps, qui sert à convertir le temps vrai en temps moyen.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L015}{}{}{« Ce procédé est au fond le même que celui de Ptolémée, à la réserve que le précepte de}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L016}{}{}{« Ptolémée donne la différence de deux \textit{équations} du temps, au lieu que la règle moderne ne}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L017}{}{}{« donne qu’une seule \textit{équation.} Avec nos tables d’\textit{équation du temps} nous corrigerions sé-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L018}{}{}{« parément les deux temps donnés, et nous prendrions la différence des deux temps corrigés,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L019}{}{}{« qui seroit l’intervalle exprimé en temps moyens ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0312-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L020}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Tannery}, \textit{Recherches sur l’astronomie ancienne,} Paris 1893, p. 178, ait methodum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L021}{}{}{Ptolemaicam usque ad \Name{Tychonem Brahe} viguisse. Cfr. \Name{Riccioli}, \textit{Alm. novum,} I, 177-78.}
\par\vspace{2.94mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0312-H22}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L022}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{d}}) — \textit{Pag. 49, l. 25-34.}}}
\par\vspace{2.25mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0312-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L023}{}{}{\Indent [Dictio huius loci perobscura. Primum vult \textit{tempus medium, in methodo qua ipse usus}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L024}{}{}{\textit{sit, semper minus futurum esse tempore quod locus Solis indicet,} id est tempore vero;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L025}{}{}{haec patent ex iis quae in adn. \textbf{\textit{b}}, p. 221–22 dixi. — Addit postea \textit{id futurum esse per lon-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L026}{}{}{\textit{gissimum tempus,} eo usque quo \textit{motus apogei solaris} e praecessione oriens \textit{mutaverit} sensi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L027}{}{}{bili modo \textit{processum illius aequationis partis quae ex anomalia motus solaris provenit.} —}
\par\vspace{-0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L028}{}{}{Deinde loquitur de 18 minutis quibus longitudinem Lunae in epocha tabularum auxit; quorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L029}{}{}{causam in adn. \textbf{\textit{b}}, p. 222, exposui. — Demum, quae praecedunt confirmans, indicat aequatio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L030}{}{}{nem suae tabulae \textit{subtrahendam semper} esse tempori vero ut medium exeat, et medio \textit{ad-}}
\par\vspace{0.15mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L031}{}{}{\textit{dendam} ut verum tempus inveniatur.}
\par\vspace{-0.17mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0312-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L032}{}{}{\Indent Verba « \textit{ad terminum longi spatii temporis, in quo variatio apogei Solis, quod in}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L033}{}{}{« \textit{ecliptica invenimus, crescet; qua re mutabitur quod ex inaequalitate motus Solis pro-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L034}{}{}{« \textit{venit} » duo magni momenti inventa indicant. Alterum, quod iam dixi, \textit{aequationem temporis}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L035}{}{}{\textit{in annos lente cum motu saeculari apogei mutari;} alterum, de quo in adn. \textbf{\textit{c}} ad XXVIII}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L036}{}{}{cap. (pag. 216–219) dissertum est, \textit{falsam esse Ptolemaei sententiam de immutabili apogei}}
\par\vspace{0.11mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L037}{}{}{\textit{solaris distantia ab aequinoctio.} — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\fontsize{15}{17}\selectfont
\par\vspace{4.34mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0312-H38}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L038}{}{}{\CenterLine{A\textsc{d} \textsc{caput} XXX.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.84mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0312-H39}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L039}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{a}}) — \textit{Pag. 50—pag. 51, l. 27.}}}
\par\vspace{4.37mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0312-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L040}{}{}{\Indent Hucusque omnia ex \textit{Almag.} V, 2 (ed. Halma t. I, p. 288–292) sumpta sunt. Figuram ta-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L041}{}{}{men et demonstrationem pag. 51, in codice et apud Platonem corruptas, facile in versione}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0312-body-L042}{}{}{minimis mutationibus sanavi, \Name{Schiaparelli} duce. Figura codicis et Platonis haec est:}
\DSource{0313}{224}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr -3.43mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\par\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.384\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0313-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0313-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.596\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.404\FullSourceWidth}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.925}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0313-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent [Dum quae in pag. 52–54 scripta legun-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L002}{}{}{tur satis bene congruunt cum \textit{Almagesti} do-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L003}{}{}{ctrina, figura et demonstratio p. 51 (quales co-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L004}{}{}{dex et Plato habent) falsae sunt et contrariae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L005}{}{}{iis quae postea exponuntur et figurae p. 53. Ma-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L006}{}{}{nifeste scriptor archetypi unde codex Escuria-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L007}{}{}{lensis et codex Platonis orti sunt, cum negle-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L008}{}{}{genter figuram exarasset, stulte ei voluit de-}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L009}{}{}{monstrationem accommodare. — Quid circulus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L010}{}{}{sit qui, centrum in F habens, per B et D tran-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L011}{}{}{sit, intelligi non potest; inutilis prorsus. Deest}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L012}{}{}{autem punctum maximi momenti in theoria}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L013}{}{}{Ptolemaei, quod in figura correcta designatur}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L014}{}{}{littera F′; a Terra E distat quanto, ex oppo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L015}{}{}{sita parte, centrum F excentrici, et indicat}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L016}{}{}{directionem veram apogei H epicycli, unde de-}
\end{minipage}\par
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{-0.42mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L017}{}{}{ducitur calculus arcus GH. Pro F′ consideratur ipsum centrum excentrici, et duabus rectis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L018}{}{}{FM, EM definitur arcus quidam GH, qui falso ponitur esse arcus GH figurae pag. 53. Similis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L019}{}{}{error in demonstratione. Parvis correctionibus p. 51, l. 13–18 factis, textus \Name{al–Battānī} bene}
\par\vspace{0.12mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L020}{}{}{restituitur. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{5.23mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0313-H21}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L021}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{b}}) — \textit{Pag. 51, l. 36—pag. 52, l. 10.}}}
\par\vspace{4.40mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0313-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L022}{}{}{\Indent Numeri omnes ex \textit{Almagesto} sumpti, ut suo loco notavi. Pro 5° 1′ inaequalitatis simplicis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L023}{}{}{auctores \textit{Tabulae Probatae} (cfr. \Name{Ibn Yūnus} cap. VI, \Name{Caussin} p. 232) et \Name{Ḥabash} f. 35,r.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L024}{}{}{et seqq., 5° tantum receperunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0313-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L025}{}{}{\Indent [Inaequalitas simplex in maxima sua quantitate (5° 1′) est \textit{minimus} valor aequationis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L026}{}{}{maximae in epicyclo; maximus, iuxta Ptolemaeum, 7° 40′. Medius 6° 20′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$ provenit; talis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L027}{}{}{esset maxima aequatio centri Lunae, nisi secunda inaequalitas, quam nos \textit{evectionem} voca-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L028}{}{}{mus, exstaret. Hodie valorem medium 6° 18′ ponunt; secundam inaequalitatem $\pm$ 1° 16′; ergo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L029}{}{}{iuxta Ptolemaeum 5° 1′, 6° 20′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$, 7° 40′; iuxta recentiores 5° 2′, 6° 18′, 7° 34′. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0313-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L030}{}{}{\Indent Tertia inaequalitas, quam \textit{variationem} vocamus, ignota fuit ante \Name{Tychonem Brahe};}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L031}{}{}{perperam eam quidam dixerunt \Name{Abū ’l–Wefā’} (m. 388 heg., inc. 2 Ian. 998 Chr.) iam inve-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L032}{}{}{nisse, ut satis demonstravit \Name{Carra de Vaux}, \textit{L’Almageste d’Aboû ’l–Wéfa al–Bûzdjâni,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L033}{}{}{p. 434–471 (in Journal Asiatique, ser. VIII, t. 19, 1892). Sed in tabulis Ptolemaei (et Arabum),}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L034}{}{}{ut primus \Name{Tannery}, \textit{Rech. sur l’astr. ancienne,} p. 214 animadvertit, praeter terminum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L035}{}{}{1° 19′ 30″ sin (2 [\MoonSym{} $-$ \SunSym{}] $-$ \textit{m}), id est praeter evectionem (1), terminus quoque 18′ sin 2 (\MoonSym{} $-$ \SunSym{})}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L036}{}{}{reperitur qui est dimidia fere pars variationis. Cfr. etiam \Name{Carra de Vaux}, p. 470.}
\par\vspace{5.29mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0313-H37}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L037}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{c}}) — \textit{Pag. 53, l. 18–20.}}}
\par\vspace{4.49mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0313-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L038}{}{}{\Indent [Verba Italicis litteris impressa, interpolata mihi videntur. Instituerat \Name{al–Battānī} antea}
\par\vspace{-1.43mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-body-L039}{}{}{calculum 42′ 38″ $= 60'\,\dfrac{1°\,53'}{2°\,40'}$; nunc, quasi res nova et miranda esset, docemur 2° 40′ $=$ 1° 53′}
\par\vspace{-0.43mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}{\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{0.22mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0313-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-notes-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Signo \MoonSym{} indico mediam Lunae longitudinem, \SunSym{} mediam longitudinem Solis, \textit{m} mediam anoma-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0313-notes-L002}{}{}{liam Lunae.}
}
\DSource{0314}{225}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr -1.07mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L001}{}{}{$\dfrac{60'}{42'\,38''}$. Hoc calculo facto (qui 2° 39′ pro 2° 40′ praebet), interpolator noster, ignorans quid}
\par\vspace{-0.43mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L002}{}{}{hic numerus sit, eum quaerit in VI columna tabulae et invenit sub argumento 110° quod}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L003}{}{}{nihil huius problematis interest; suo ipso errore implicatus, 110° in 120° mutat, ut redeat ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L004}{}{}{Ptolemaei numerum qui angulum AEM scilicet anomaliam mediam excentrici metitur. At in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L005}{}{}{tabula numero 120° res prorsus diversa respondet. Manifeste auctor harum linearum nihil}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L006}{}{}{huius calculi intelligebat, et al–Battānī esse omnino nequit. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{5.39mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0314-H07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L007}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{d}}) — \textit{Pag. 54, l. 19–30.}}}
\par\vspace{3.23mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0314-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L008}{}{}{\Indent [\textit{Tabulae lunares.} — Tabulis \Name{al–Battānī} cum \Name{Ptolemaei} comparatis, quae auctor}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L009}{}{}{narrat vera apparent. Motui Lunae in longitudine addidit correctionem iam antea (cap. XXVII,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L010}{}{}{p. 42) motui Solis illatam, qua re elongatio media (\MoonSym{} $-$ \SunSym{}) restat ut apud Ptolemaeum. Mo-}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L011}{}{}{tus anomalisticus quoque immutatus.}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0314-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L012}{}{}{\Indent Motum Ptolemaicum nodi vel latitudinis (1) ait se 27′ imminutum reperisse in intervallo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L013}{}{}{750 fere annorum quod est inter eum et Ptolemaeum. Amborum tabulis inter se comparatis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L014}{}{}{invenio motum Albatenianum latitudinis minorem quidem Ptolemaico, sed non exacte ea quan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L015}{}{}{titate; certissime colligitur al–Battānī motum latitudinis tribus minutis in saeculo praecise}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L016}{}{}{minuisse, quod, in 750 annis, 22′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$ pro 27′ efficit. — Numerus tamen 27′ codicis et Platonis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L017}{}{}{certus est, nam confirmatur iis quae in lineis 28–30 narrantur. Si in 750 annis correctio}
\par\vspace{-0.97mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L018}{}{}{27′ apparuerit, in 1200 annis circiter qui ab aera Dhū ’l–qarnayn elapsi sunt correctio 27′ $\dfrac{1200}{750}$}
\par\vspace{-0.40mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L019}{}{}{$=$ 43′,2 erit. Haec sunt $\qfrac{3}{4}$ gradus quibus motus latitudinis ab epocha aerae ad « \textit{hoc no-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L020}{}{}{\textit{strum tempus} », ut al–Battānī ait, minuendus erat, ante quam epochae illius motus, notatae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L021}{}{}{in ultima columna t. II, p. 72, supputarentur.}
\par\vspace{-0.10mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0314-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L022}{}{}{\Indent Mirum quibusdam videri potest \Name{al–Battānī} correctionem motus latitudinis (vel motus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L023}{}{}{nodi) 27′ minutorum tantum (22′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$ in tabulis) in 750 annis invenisse, cum hoc temporis spatio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L024}{}{}{correctio motus longitudinis haud minor 4° 31′ evasisset. Res ita explicatur: Veteres astro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L025}{}{}{nomi motum longitudinis Lunae ad motum Solis referebant, utentes epochis eclipsium obser-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L026}{}{}{vatarum; quanto igitur motus Solis errore afficiebatur, tanto motus Lunae in eodem inter-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L027}{}{}{vallo temporis. Sed motus Solis (vel longitudo anni) ab observationibus pendebat aequinoctiorum,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L028}{}{}{quorum quaeque erratum \textit{plurium horarum,} immo \textit{dierum,} praebere poterat. — Motus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L029}{}{}{contra nodorum Lunae inveniebatur per Lunae situm respectu nodorum in iisdem eclipsibus;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L030}{}{}{hic situs autem intra \textit{pauca minuta arcus} facile determinari poterat per magnitudinem ecli-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L031}{}{}{psium observatarum.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0314-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-body-L032}{}{}{\Indent Addo comparationem tabularum lunarium \Name{Ptolemaei}, \Name{Albatenii} et \Name{Hansen} (2):}
\par\vspace{-0.60mm}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0314-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Motus latitudinis apud al–Battānī est summa}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0314-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0314-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (2) [Cuius numeri ex \textit{Monthly Notices of the}}

\DSource{0315}{226}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0315-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Al–Battānī} ergo motum longitudinis multo melius quam \Name{Ptolemaeus} determinavit;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L002}{}{}{inclinationem orbitae et motum anomaliae immutatos servavit; motum latitudinis minus bene}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L003}{}{}{correxit. Sed in hoc motu numerus Hansenii non est adeo certus, ut comparatio tuta fieri}
\par\vspace{0.22mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L004}{}{}{possit. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{-0.20mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0315-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L005}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Astronomi khalifae al–Ma’mūn} in \textit{Tabula Probata} (cfr. \Name{Ibn Yūnus} cap. VI,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L006}{}{}{\Name{Caussin} p. 232) motum longitudinis meliorem Ptolemaico iam invenerant, sed motum nodi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L007}{}{}{nimis parvum statuerant. Motus lunares in anno Persico 365 dierum (sine intercalatione)}
\par\vspace{0.13mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L008}{}{}{ita sunt:}

\par\vspace{3.30mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0315-H14}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L014}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{e}}) — \textit{Pag. 54, l. 33.}}}
\par\vspace{2.57mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0315-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L015}{}{}{\Indent « \textit{Et ultra sphaeram Mercurii desinit} ». — In hoc ipso capite, p. 61, supputat umbram}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L016}{}{}{Terrae, cum Sol in maxima distantia geocentrica sit, 254 $\qfrac{2}{3}$ radios terrestres protendi ultra}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L017}{}{}{centrum Terrae. In L capite, p. 120–121, invenit}
\par\vspace{2.26mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L018}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{28.89mm}\smash{\rlap{distantiam perigeam Mercurii}}}\rlap{\hspace*{106.20mm}\smash{\llap{64}}}\rlap{\hspace*{108.33mm}\smash{\rlap{$\qfrac{1}{6}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{115.34mm}\smash{\rlap{radiorum terrestrium}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L019}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{28.89mm}\smash{\rlap{distantiam apogeam Mercurii}}}\rlap{\hspace*{91.24mm}\smash{\rlap{\smash{\raisebox{-16.6pt}{\tikz\draw[line width=.55pt,decorate,decoration={brace,amplitude=2.3pt}] (0,25.8pt) -- (0,0);}}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{106.20mm}\smash{\raisebox{-.55\baselineskip}{\llap{166}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{122.75mm}\smash{\raisebox{-.55\baselineskip}{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{144.59mm}\smash{\raisebox{-.55\baselineskip}{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L020}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{28.89mm}\smash{\rlap{distantiam perigeam Veneris}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L021}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{28.89mm}\smash{\rlap{distantiam apogeam Veneris}}}\rlap{\hspace*{106.55mm}\smash{\llap{1070}}}\rlap{\hspace*{122.75mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}\rlap{\hspace*{144.59mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}}
\par\vspace{2.60mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L022}{}{}{Unde consequitur in systemate caelesti Albateniano umbram Terrae sphaeram Mercurii trans-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L023}{}{}{gredi et in ipsam Veneris sphaeram ingredi.}
\par\vspace{5.36mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0315-H24}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L024}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{f}}) — \textit{Pag. 55, l. 15.}}}
\par\vspace{4.37mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0315-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L025}{}{}{\Indent [Mercurius et Venus nunquam contra Solem oppositi cernuntur; hinc statim intelligitur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L026}{}{}{cur neuter planeta in umbram Terrae incidere queat. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{5.17mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0315-H27}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L027}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{g}}) — \textit{Pag. 56, l. 9–21.}}}
\par\vspace{4.45mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0315-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L028}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Prima eclipsis} (Solis), die 8 āb (Augusti) 1202 a Dhū ’l–qarnayn, 891 Chr., hora 1\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 7\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} $\qfrac{1}{2}$}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L029}{}{}{in urbe ar–Raqqah (2) observata. Numerum 4995 fert apud \Name{Oppolzer}, \textit{Canon der Finster-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-body-L030}{}{}{\textit{nisse} (Denkschriften der k. Akad. der Wissenschaften zu Wien, Mathem. Cl., t. LII, 1887).}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{-0.15mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0315-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Hi numeri, usque ad sextas supputati, sunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-notes_left-L002}{}{}{in \textit{Almag.} IV, 3 (ed. Halma t. I, p. 222-25, et tabulae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-notes_left-L003}{}{}{p. 226-237). Motus nodi ibi non indicatur, sed statim}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-notes_left-L004}{}{}{elicitur motum medium longitudinis de motu medio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-notes_left-L005}{}{}{latitudinis demendo.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{-0.17mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0315-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (2) Observationibus astronomicis anno 1835 fa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-notes_right-L002}{}{}{ctis \Name{Chesney} urbis longitudinem 39° 3′ 57″ E Gr.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-notes_right-L003}{}{}{statuit, latitudinem 35° 55′ 35″ N; locus observationis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-notes_right-L004}{}{}{fuit angulus N–W ruinarum palatii olim a khalifa}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0315-notes_right-L005}{}{}{Hārūn ar–Rashīd exstructi.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0316}{227}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0316-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0316-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Halley} in dissertatione p. 56, adn. 2 indicata, elementa omnium harum eclipsium innu-}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0316-body-L002}{}{}{meris erroribus editionum Platonis purgare conatus est, cum radices mediorum motuum,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0316-body-L003}{}{}{quantum fieri poterat emendatas, e capitibus XXVII, XXVIII, XXX collexisset. Multa feli-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0316-body-L004}{}{}{citer restituit. Sed emendationes tantum indicavit, calculis quibus eas invenerat omissis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0316-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0316-body-L005}{}{}{\Indent [Ad numeros codicis et Platonis rite corrigendos, auctoris supputationes ex eiusdem ta-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0316-body-L006}{}{}{bulis et hypothesibus repetendae erunt. Ac primum ait al–Battānī tempus coniunctionis verae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0316-body-L007}{}{}{octava parte circiter unius horae coniunctionem apparentem seu medium eclipsis antecessisse}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0316-body-L008}{}{}{(vide p. 56, l. 16–17); unde sequitur tempus coniunctionis verae 1\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 0\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} post meridiem. Praeterea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0316-body-L009}{}{}{dubium non est Albatenii tempus, horis temporariis in observando designatum, \textit{verum} esse,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0316-body-L010}{}{}{non \textit{medium}. Sole 19° Leonis gradum occupante, ex tabula Albateniana (t. II, p. 63) prodit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0316-body-L011}{}{}{aequatio nychthemerōn 3° 16′ $=$ 13\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} 4\textsuperscript{\textit{s}}, nempe subtrahenda; hinc tempus medium coniunctio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0316-body-L012}{}{}{nis verae sive geocentricae 0\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 47\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} post meridiem, missis aliquot temporis secundis, in hac re}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0316-body-L013}{}{}{prorsus contemnendis. Calculus itaque, e tabularum tomo p. 72–83 petitus, ita se habet:}
\par\vspace{4.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0316-body-L014}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{26.51mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{Motus medii}}}\rlap{\hspace*{64.50mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{Long.\SunSym{} media}}}\rlap{\hspace*{98.12mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{Long.\MoonSym{} media}}}\rlap{\hspace*{131.90mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{Anom.\MoonSym{} med.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{165.34mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{Nodus asc. \MoonSym{}}}}}
\par\vspace{-0.61mm}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0317-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent Elementa eclipsis hoc modo deducta in I columna tabellae hic infra appositae collexi; at}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0317-body-L002}{}{}{iisdem uti non licuit pro instituto nostro. Nam al–Battānī longitudinem Lunae e tabulis suis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0317-body-L003}{}{}{non sumpsit; immo posuit, quod ex observatione certum erat, longitudines veras Solis et Lu-}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0317-body-L004}{}{}{nae aequales esse. Itaque pro Luna 139° 14′ accepit loco 139° 11′ 30″ quae tabulae dabant.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0317-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0317-body-L005}{}{}{\Indent Sequentis tabellae columna II exhibet numeros quos al–Battānī ex hac sua hypothesi invenire}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0317-body-L006}{}{}{debuit; quos cum codicis et Platonis numeris, in columnis III et IV expositis, comparare nunc}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0317-body-L007}{}{}{fas erit. Adiectae sunt quoque, in columna V, Halleyi emendationes, quarum supra mentio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0317-body-L008}{}{}{facta est.}
\par\vspace{-1.56mm}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0317-body-L021}{}{}{codici, scilicet \ArabicRun{قلز} 137° pro \ArabicRun{قلد} 134° in longitudine media Lunae, et \ArabicRun{سلج يج} 333° 13′ pro \ArabicRun{سلب نز}}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0317-body-L025}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Secunda eclipsis} (Solis) die 22 kānūn posterioris 1212 Dhū ’l–qarnayn, i. e. 22 Ianuarii}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0317-body-L026}{}{}{901 Chr., 20\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} $\qfrac{1}{8}$ fere in urbe Antiochia (1) observata. — In canone \Name{Oppolzer} numerum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0317-body-L027}{}{}{5016 fert.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0317-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0317-body-L028}{}{}{\Indent [Meridies, de qua al–Battānī loquitur, certe meridies \textit{vera} est seu culminatio Solis (2).}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0317-body-L029}{}{}{Antiochiae igitur 20\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 20\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} fuit tempus \textit{verum} observationis, in urbe ar–Raqqah 20\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 30\textsuperscript{\textit{m}}, vel}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0317-body-L030}{}{}{paulo plus, ut al–Battānī vult. Hinc eruitur tempus \textit{medium} ar–Raqqah 20\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 29\textsuperscript{\textit{m}}, cum aequatio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0317-body-L031}{}{}{nychthemerōn pro octavo Aquarii gradu 15′ $=$ 1\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} inveniatur (vide tabulas in t. II, p. 61). Ait}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0317-body-L032}{}{}{autem coniunctionem veram 50\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} posterius evenisse; eius coniunctionis tempus medium igitur}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0317-body-L033}{}{}{fuit 21\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 19\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} pro meridiano ar–Raqqah vel paulo plus. Qua re pro 21\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 20\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} calculis ducere}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0317-body-L034}{}{}{oportebit.}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0318-body-L022}{}{}{\Indent Momenta eclipsis hoc calculo reperta in columna I sequentis tabellae inscripta sunt. Lon-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0318-body-L023}{}{}{gitudines verae Solis et Lunae paucis tantum secundis inter se differunt; unde patet tempus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0318-body-L024}{}{}{coniunctionis verae ex observatione derivatum optime cum tabulis Albatenianis consentire.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0318-body-L025}{}{}{Al–Battānī tamen utriusque sideris longitudinem veram 308° 35′ sive uno fere minuto mino-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0318-body-L026}{}{}{rem posuit; qua hypothesi accepta, eius computationes in columna II restituere conatus sum.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0318-body-L027}{}{}{Demum in tribus columnis sequentibus numeros codicis, Platonis et Halleyi ad comparationem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0318-body-L028}{}{}{instituendam adieci.}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0319-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0319-body-L002}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Tertia eclipsis} (Lunae), die 23 Tammūz 1194 a Dhū ’l–qarnayn, i. e. 23 Iulii 883 Chr.,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0319-body-L003}{}{}{perpaulo magis quam 8 horis aequinoctialibus post meridiem observata in urbe ar–Raqqah.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0319-body-L004}{}{}{Numerum 3232 in canone \Name{Oppolzer} fert.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0319-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0319-body-L005}{}{}{\Indent [Sole Leonis gradus 4° 2′ occupante, aequatio nychthemerōn 3° 4′ sive 12\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} 16\textsuperscript{\textit{s}} subtra-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0319-body-L006}{}{}{henda invenitur; unde tempus verum mediae eclipsis, idemque oppositionis verae perpaulo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0319-body-L007}{}{}{magis quam 7\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 48\textsuperscript{\textit{m}}. Cuius temporis ope in Albatenianas tabulas ingressus, numeros ubique}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0319-body-L008}{}{}{Albatenianis minores nactus sum. Re autem attentius perpensa, vidi vocabulum \textit{perpaulo} ita}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0319-body-L009}{}{}{posse interpretari, ut loco 7\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 48\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} libentius 7\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 55\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} assumantur.}
\par\vspace{3.68mm}

\par\vspace{3.80mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0321-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L011}{}{}{\Indent In codice Escurialensi ergo sunt gradus longitudinis mediae Solis (\ArabicRun{قلو} 136° pro \ArabicRun{قلد} 134°),}
\par\vspace{0.41mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L012}{}{}{minuta anomaliae mediae (\ArabicRun{يا} 11′ pro \ArabicRun{د} 4′) et minuta anomaliae verae (\ArabicRun{ط} 9′ pro \ArabicRun{ه} 5′, error}
\par\vspace{0.45mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L013}{}{}{in scriptura maghrebina facilis) corrigenda. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0321-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L014}{}{}{\Indent II. — \textit{Animadversiones generales ad has quatuor eclipses.}}
\par\vspace{-0.10mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0321-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L015}{}{}{\Indent [De his quatuor eclipsibus ab Albatenio observatis et in calculum relatis, pauca adiicere}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L016}{}{}{opportunum est; ac primum de earum cum tabulis Albatenianis optimo consensu. Tabella se-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L017}{}{}{quens exhibet primo loco pro unaquaque eclipsi tempus medium coniunctionis verae ex obser-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L018}{}{}{vatione, ad meridianum urbis ar–Raqqah. Secundo et tertio loco longitudines veras Solis et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L019}{}{}{Lunae secundum tabulas, pro eodem momento coniunctionis (aut oppositionis) verae; quae lon-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L020}{}{}{gitudines sane aequales esse deberent (aut 180° gradibus inter se differre), si nullus error in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L021}{}{}{observationem et in tabulas irrepsisset. Quarto loco exhibet differentiam \MoonSym{}$-$\SunSym{}, quae dat er-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L022}{}{}{rorem elongationis \MoonSym{} e tabulis procedentem. Demum differentiam inter tempus coniunctionis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L023}{}{}{(aut oppositionis) verae ex observatione petitum (quod littera O designo) et tempus coniun-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L024}{}{}{ctionis (aut oppositionis) verae e tabulis derivatum (quod littera T noto).}
\par\vspace{0.51mm}

\par\vspace{3.71mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0321-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L031}{}{}{\Indent Errores \MoonSym{}$-$\SunSym{} et O$-$T profecto sunt pro minimis habendi; immo multo minores viden-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L032}{}{}{tur quam exspectandum esset in observationibus sine telescopio habitis, subsidiis etiam ad ac-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L033}{}{}{curatam temporis definitionem tunc deficientibus. Qua re suspicio subit al–Battānī ad harum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L034}{}{}{eclipsium normam proprias tabulas emendasse.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0321-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L035}{}{}{\Indent Res aliter se habet si quatuor eclipses cum tabulis recentioribus Lunae comparemus.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L036}{}{}{Theoria Lunae, astronomorum nostri aevi maximis laboribus quamvis exculta, eo tamen cer-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L037}{}{}{titudinis nondum pervenit ut ad eius rationem veteres Babyloniorum, Graecorum et Arabum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L038}{}{}{observationes tuto diiudicare liceat. Quin immo, ex observationibus illis theoriae quodammodo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L039}{}{}{opem ferre paucis abhinc annis vir omni laude maior, Theodorus \Name{Oppolzer}, in tabulis suis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0321-body-L040}{}{}{syzygiarum, praesertim vero in magno \textit{Canone eclipsium} strenua opera conatus est. Ean-}
\DSource{0322}{233}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L001}{}{}{dem viam postea ingressus est F. K. \Name{Ginzel}, qui, antiquis eclipsium totalium Solis usus ob-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L002}{}{}{servationibus, novas Canonis Oppolzeriani correctiones enucleare diligentissime studuit (1); quas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L003}{}{}{demum tabellis optime ad usum astronomorum accommodatis expressit R. \Name{Schram} (2). Sic}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L004}{}{}{coniunctis trium astronomorum laboribus tabulae exstiterunt, partim theoretico, partim empi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L005}{}{}{rico fundamento innixae, quae inter omnes accuratissime cursum Lunae pro antiquis tempo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L006}{}{}{ribus exhibere censendae sunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0322-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L007}{}{}{\Indent Ad comparationem nostrarum eclipsium cum his tabulis recte instituendam primum me-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L008}{}{}{minisse oportet, tempus medium Albatenii non idem esse ac tempus medium nobis usitatum.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L009}{}{}{Iam antea, in adn. \textbf{\textit{b}} ad XXIX caput (p. 221–22), demonstratum est Albatenio aequationem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L010}{}{}{nychthemerōn semper esse subtractivam, Ptolemaeo additivam, ita ut tempori medio Albatenii}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L011}{}{}{circiter 32\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} addenda sint, ut ad Ptolemaei usum reducatur. Modus vero, quo nostrates aequa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L012}{}{}{tionem illam computare solent, ita est comparatus ut inter Albatenianam et Ptolemaicam me-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L013}{}{}{thodum quam proxime medium teneat. Quare, ne nimis perplexis iisdemque non necessariis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L014}{}{}{calculis involvamur, pro instituto nostro sat erit tempori medio Albatenii dimidium intervalli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L015}{}{}{nuper dicti, scilicet 16\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} addere, ut numeri auctoris ad usum nostrum revocentur, quem Cano-}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L016}{}{}{nis Oppolzeriani auctores quoque secuti sunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0322-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L017}{}{}{\Indent Altera reductio est reductio longitudinis, a meridianis urbium Antiochiae et ar–Raqqah,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L018}{}{}{in quibus observatum est, ad meridianum Grenovicensem, quo usi sunt Oppolzer eiusque socii.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L019}{}{}{Longitudinem orientalem a Grenovico pro Antiochia (ubi II et IV eclipsis observatae sunt)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L020}{}{}{sumpsi 36° 9′ $=$ 2\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 24\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} 36\textsuperscript{\textit{s}} e Hartmanni auctoritate; pro ar–Raqqah (ubi I et III eclipsis ob-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L021}{}{}{servatae) 39° 4′ $=$ 2\textsuperscript{\textit{h}} 36\textsuperscript{\textit{m}} 16\textsuperscript{\textit{s}} iuxta observationes Chesney.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0322-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L022}{}{}{\Indent Hisce perpensis, in sequenti tabella elementa comparationis collexi.}
\par\vspace{2.34mm}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0322-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L029}{}{}{\Indent Statim colligitur in omnibus eclipsibus syzygiam ex observatione quadrante circiter horae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L030}{}{}{serius poni, quam tabulae ferant. Huius differentiae partem aliquam observationibus ipsis esse}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L031}{}{}{tribuendam non dubito. Initium enim obscurationis oculo nudo percipi non potest, nisi aliquot}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L032}{}{}{minuta temporis a geometrico contactu sint elapsa. Id autem praesertim evenire debuit anti-}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L033}{}{}{quis astronomis, quibus initium eclipsis e tabulis satis accurate praedici non poterat. Haec}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-body-L034}{}{}{incertitudo in notando obscurationis fine locum non habuit; qua re in aestimando medio ecli-}
\par\vspace{0.45mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{0.12mm}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) \textit{Astronomische Untersuchungen über Fin-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\textit{sternisse}, in Sitzungsberichte der mathem.-natur-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-notes_left-L003}{}{}{wissenschaftliche Classe der k. Akad. der WW. zu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-notes_left-L004}{}{}{Wien, tom. LXXXV, sectio II, 1882, pag. 663-747;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-notes_left-L005}{}{}{t. LXXXVIII, s. II, 1883, p. 629-755; t. LXXXIX, s. II,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-notes_left-L006}{}{}{1884;. p. 491-559.}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0322-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (2) \textit{Reductionstafeln für den Oppolzer‘sche}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\textit{Finsterniss-Canon, zum Uebergang auf die Gin-}}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\textit{zel’schen empirischen Correctionen}, in Denkschrif-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-notes_right-L004}{}{}{ten der k. Akad. der WW. zu Wien, mathem. Classe,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0322-notes_right-L005}{}{}{t. LVI, 1889.}

\par\vspace{5mm}\hfill{\fontsize{8}{10}\selectfont 30}\hspace{10mm}
\DSource{0323}{234}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L001}{}{}{psis nonnihil retardari necesse fuit. An vero totus error integri horae quadrantis huic causae}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L002}{}{}{sit adscribendus, affirmare non audeo; ipse enim al–Battānī, rudiori modo, tempora sua non}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L003}{}{}{accuratius quam per denorum minutorum intervalla adnotasse videtur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L004}{}{}{\Indent Demum \textit{magnitudines} eclipsium lunarium ab al–Battānī observatarum cum Oppolzeri}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L005}{}{}{supputationibus comparare iuvabit. Magnitudo eclipsis III, quam al–Battānī 10 digitorum aesti-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L006}{}{}{mavit, fuit iuxta Oppolzer 11,2 digitorum. Magnitudo eclipsis IV, quam al–Battānī 12 digitorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L007}{}{}{vel paulo minorem fuisse vidit, e calculis Oppolzer nonnihilo maior, seu 12,6 digitorum inve-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L008}{}{}{nitur. — Magnitudines duarum eclipsium Solis, I et II, al–Battānī non tradidit. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{4.14mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-H09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L009}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{l}}) — \textit{Pag. 58, l. 1–4.}}}
\par\vspace{3.37mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L010}{}{}{\Indent [« \textit{Sol ad apogeum versus erat} ». — Nam in III eclipsi anomalia \SunSym{} 42° erat, in IV 52°.}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.144\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0323-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.836\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.164\FullSourceWidth}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.749}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L011}{}{}{\Indent « \textit{Luna eundem locum occupabat partibus 1\textsuperscript{p} $\qfrac{1}{2}$ circiter minorem}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L012}{}{}{\textit{quam distantiam mediam} ». — I. e. 1\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} $\qfrac{1}{2}$ circiter ex 60 partes in quas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L013}{}{}{media distantia (AE in figura pag. 51) Lunae coniunctae et oppositae Soli di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L014}{}{}{vidi statuitur. Re vera in III eclipsi anomalia vera Lunae in epicyclo 114° 16′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L015}{}{}{erat, in IV 111° 4′; et hae sunt quantitates anguli QAL in figura hoc loco}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L016}{}{}{descripta, ubi E terra est, A centrum epicycli, AE $=$ 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}, AL $=$ 5\textsuperscript{\textit{p}},247. Cal-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L017}{}{}{culus trianguli AEL, secundum III eclipsim, EL $=$58\textsuperscript{\textit{p}},04 praebet; secundum IV,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L018}{}{}{EL $=$ 58\textsuperscript{\textit{p}},32. Quod ergo « 1\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} $\qfrac{1}{2}$ circiter » \Name{al–Battānī} dicit erat exacte 1\textsuperscript{\textit{p}},96}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L019}{}{}{et 1\textsuperscript{\textit{p}},68; manifeste calculum adproximate instituit.}
\par\vspace{0.12mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L020}{}{}{\Indent « \textit{Inter primam ac alteram latitudinem 0° 3′ 50″ intercedebant} ». —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L021}{}{}{Iuxta numeros Albatenianos prima latitudo (III ecl.) « 0° 32′ circiter in meri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L022}{}{}{« diem versus fuit »; altera (IV ecl.) « 0° 28′ circiter » (plagam indicare omi-}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L023}{}{}{sit, sed ex motu latitudinis 185° 21′ patet australem quoque fuisse). Differentia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L024}{}{}{ergo 4′ pro 3′ 50″. Sed supputatio exactior a me instituta, pro duabus illis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L025}{}{}{latitudinibus 0° 31′ 52″ et 0° 28′ 3″ praebuit; differentia 3′ 49″, bene cum Al-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L026}{}{}{batenianis 3′ 50″ congruens. Manifeste \Name{al–Battānī} eclipsium calculos praecisius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L027}{}{}{duxerat; postea in libro numeros rotundos reddidit. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\end{minipage}\par
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{-1.37mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-H28}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L028}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{m}}) — \textit{Pag. 58, l. 4–5.}}}
\par\vspace{2.99mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L029}{}{}{\Indent « \textit{Diametro lunari differentia duarum eclipsium} 8 $\qfrac{3}{4}$ \textit{vicibus continetur} ». — Cum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L030}{}{}{observationibus auctoris non concordat. In III eclipsi pars obscurata $\qfrac{5}{6}$ diametri fuit; in IV}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L031}{}{}{Luna fere tota. Differentia 1 $-$ $\qfrac{5}{6}$ $=$ $\qfrac{1}{6}$. Ergo pro 8 $\qfrac{3}{4}$ debebat 6 scribere.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L032}{}{}{\Indent [Puto \Name{al–Battānī}, ne ad absurdam diametrum lunarem vix 23 minuta attingentem (ut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L033}{}{}{e factore 6 necessarie oriebatur) duceretur, quodam modo ad numericam rationem revocare}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L034}{}{}{voluisse illa « paululo amplius $\qfrac{5}{6}$ » tertiae eclipsis, et « perpaulo minus quam sua diametro »}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L035}{}{}{quartae. Cum vero haec numeris indicare difficile esset, factorem 8 $\qfrac{3}{4}$ delegisse, diametrum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L036}{}{}{lunarem 33′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$ aestimando. Solummodo Albatenius (parum perspicue, ut videtur) ostendere vo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L037}{}{}{luit, Lunae diametrum a se propositam cum his duabus eclipsibus satis posse conciliari.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L038}{}{}{\Indent Ergo quae \Name{al–Battānī} dicit non debent vera diametri lunaris determinatio censeri, sed}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L039}{}{}{probatio tantum non repugnare observationes contra diametrum quam receperat. — \textsc{Schia-}}
\par\vspace{0.16mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L040}{}{}{\textsc{parelli}.]}
\par\vspace{-0.48mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-H41}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L041}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{n}}) — \textit{Pag. 58, l. 9–11.}}}
\par\vspace{2.34mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-P08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L042}{}{}{\Indent [« \textit{Lunae motus horarius temporibus syzygiarum} ..... ». — Hi motus horarii, maxi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L043}{}{}{mus 36′ 10″ et minimus 30′ 12″ (Plato 15″, nam saepissime apud eum 2 fit 5), cum tabula}
\DSource{0324}{235}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L001}{}{}{t. II, p. 88 non conveniunt, e qua 36′ 4″ et 30′ 18″ deducuntur. Causam differentiae nescio;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L002}{}{}{fortasse supputatio Albateniana adproximate tantum ducta est. — In sequentibus calculis po-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L003}{}{}{nit maximam diametrum esse ad minimam ut maximus motus horarius ad minimum. Verum}
\par\vspace{0.11mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L004}{}{}{esset si motus horarius \textit{anomaliae} aequaretur motui \textit{longitudinis}, ut in Sole evenit; error}
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L005}{}{}{tamen etiam in Lunae diametro videtur negligendus. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{8.13mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0324-H06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L006}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{o}}) — \textit{Pag. 58, l. 16–18.}}}
\par\vspace{6.42mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0324-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L007}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Semidiametrum umbrae}, cum Luna apogea sit, 0° 38′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$ fere statuit; cum Luna peri-}
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L008}{}{}{gea exstet, 0° 46′ circiter; differentia ergo 0° 7′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$ circiter.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0324-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L009}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Ibn Yūnus} (introd., \Name{Caussin} p. 70–72) haec observat: « Errant quoque Abū ’l–‘Abbās}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L010}{}{}{« al–Faḍl ibn Ḥātim \Name{an–Nayrīzī} (1) et Muḥammad ibn Ǵābir \Name{al–Battānī} cum ponunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L011}{}{}{« semidiametrum umbrae inter maximam et minimam [Lunae] distantiam variare 7′ 12″. Qui-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L012}{}{}{« cumque minus quam duo viri illi in studiis geometriae versatus agnoscet eam sententiam}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L013}{}{}{« falsam esse, si consideraverit demonstrationem \Name{Ptolemaei} [\textit{Almag.} V, 15] de distantia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L014}{}{}{« Solis a centro Terrae. Impossibile est variationem semidiametri umbrae inter distantiam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L015}{}{}{« apogeam Lunae et distantiam perigeam tanti esse quanti illi faciunt, neque 8′ vel 9′ minu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L016}{}{}{« torum; multo maior evadere debet si in hoc statuendo viam demonstrationis geometricae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L017}{}{}{« sequeris. Olim calculum ego institueram, et inveneram 0° 10′ 17″ ponens Solem et Lunam (2)}
\par\vspace{0.13mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L018}{}{}{« in maxima distantia a Terra. Variatio quae semidiametrum umbrae afficit ob proximitatem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L019}{}{}{« Solis Terrae, non potest 1 minutum excedere » (3).}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{143.41mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0324-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L020}{}{}{\Indent [Quae \Name{Ibn Yūnus} dicit hoc modo demonstrantur. Sit RN radius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L021}{}{}{umbrae in minima distantia, quem angulus RMN in minutis et secundis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L022}{}{}{metitur; R′N′ radius umbrae in maxima distantia, eiusque mensura an-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L023}{}{}{gulus R′MN′. Est KRM maxima Lunae parallaxis, iuxta al–Battānī in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L024}{}{}{syzygiis 64′ 51″ complectens; KR′M minima parallaxis, iuxta al–Bat-}
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L025}{}{}{tānī 53′ 34″. Sit $\varphi$ angulus KVM in vertice coni umbrae. In triangulo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L026}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.75mm}\smash{\rlap{MRV sunt}}}\rlap{\hspace*{41.11mm}\smash{\rlap{$KRM$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{56.97mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{60.21mm}\smash{\rlap{64′ 51″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{78.45mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{83.23mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$\varphi$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{88.00mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$+$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{91.64mm}\smash{\rlap{$RMV$}}}}
\par\vspace{0.17mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L027}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{41.78mm}\smash{\rlap{$KR'M$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{57.56mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{60.65mm}\smash{\rlap{53′ 34″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{79.03mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{83.61mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$\varphi$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{88.31mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$+$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{92.00mm}\smash{\rlap{$R'MV$}}}}
\par\vspace{1.66mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L028}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{37.52mm}\smash{\rlap{differentia}}}\rlap{\hspace*{61.45mm}\smash{\rlap{$\overline{\text{10′ 17″}}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{79.56mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{83.12mm}\smash{\rlap{$RMV$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{97.59mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$-$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{101.28mm}\smash{\rlap{$R'MV$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{116.93mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{120.64mm}\smash{\rlap{$RMR'$,}}}}
\par\vspace{3.13mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L029}{}{}{qui RMR′ est, in angulo, differentia inter maximam et minimam um-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L030}{}{}{bram. Manifeste angulus 10′ 17″ semper idem est, licet distantia Solis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L031}{}{}{et angulus coni umbrae varient. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0324-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L032}{}{}{\Indent Numeros suos manifeste \Name{al–Battānī} hoc modo invenit. Putavit}
\par\vspace{-0.11mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L033}{}{}{(cfr. p. 58, l. 7–8) rationem diametri umbrae ad diametrum Lunae esse}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L034}{}{}{quam \Name{Ptolemaeus}, \textit{Almag.} V, 14 (ed. Halma t. I, p. 343) habet, sci-}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{42.59mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0324-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0324-F01}\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{-0.37mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L035}{}{}{licet ut 2 $\qfrac{3}{5}$ ad 1; hac ratione e diametris Albatenianis maxima et minima Lunae (35′ $\qfrac{1}{3}$ et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L036}{}{}{29′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$) deducuntur diametri umbrae 91′,858 et 76′,7, semidiametri itaque 45′ 54″ et 38′ 42″.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L037}{}{}{Differentia 7′ 12″, quam \Name{Ibn Yūnus} memorat; \Name{al–Battānī} 7′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$ fere scribit, quoniam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L038}{}{}{semidiametros rotunde 46′ et 38′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$ ponit.}
\par\vspace{2.22mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0324-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Anaritius apud Latinos aevi medii scripto-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-notes_left-L002}{}{}{res. Obiit anno circiter 922 Chr.; \Name{Suter}, \textit{Die Ma-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\textit{thematiker und Astronomen}, p. 45, nr. 88.}

\par\vspace{1.12mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0324-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (2) [Melius: Solem apogeum, Lunam in apogeo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-notes_right-L002}{}{}{excentrici. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{-0.87mm}
